Check your vehicle & ECU.

Browse application coverage for selected tuning options. A matching make and control-unit family is a starting point: the exact firmware, hardware, vehicle condition and intended use still need checking. These entries do not identify a guaranteed service for every vehicle or confirm an individual software licence.

Contact us with your vehicle details and ECU or gearbox identification before booking. Some options are limited to specialist or motorsport applications. Required safety and emissions functions must be retained on road vehicles.

Coverage checked 26 October 2026. Beta and application-specific restrictions are retained. 3742 application entries across 13 option groups. Gearbox remapping requires a separate compatibility check.
